## Attacks on health care by month, 2017 on: attacks, killed, injured, what was hit (WHO SSA)

الاعتداءات على الرعاية الصحية شهرياً من 2017: الاعتداءات والشهداء والجرحى وما أصيب (منظمة الصحة العالمية)

- **Key:** `who_ssa_monthly` · **publisher:** WHO SSA (`who_ssa`)
- **Rows:** 2,912 held · 2,912 a question can reach · 2,912 exportable · 0 older values kept
- **Span:** 2017-07-01 → 2026-10-01 · **series:** 26 · **cadence:** daily
- **Freshness:** fresh: read on its own rhythm and within its allowance (last information 2026-10-01)
- **Terms:** WHO-SSA-terms · partners: yes (attribution) · terms: https://extranet.who.int/ssa/Index.aspx
- **Credit:** Attacks on health care: WHO Surveillance System for Attacks on Health Care (SSA).
- **Scope:** WHO's surveillance of attacks on health care in the oPt, counted by month from its incident records: attacks, killed, injured, attacks by what was hit and by type (an attack under each it carries), health workers and patients abducted, arrested or detained. No West Bank / Gaza split. The newest month is partial.
- **Read before use:** Counted by month from WHO's Surveillance System for Attacks on Health Care (SSA) for the occupied Palestinian territory: the attacks WHO recorded, by the month of the attack. WHO gives no West Bank / Gaza split and no place below the territory. These are attacks on health care as WHO defines them, not every health worker or hospital harmed in the war: the Ministry of Health and others count differently. WHO can withdraw a record at any time, so a month can fall. The newest month is partial until it ends (partial_month).
- **Read before use:** Counted by month from WHO's Surveillance System for Attacks on Health Care (SSA) for the occupied Palestinian territory: the attacks WHO recorded, by the month of the attack. WHO gives no West Bank / Gaza split and no place below the territory. These are attacks on health care as WHO defines them, not every health worker or hospital harmed in the war: the Ministry of Health and others count differently. WHO can withdraw a record at any time, so a month can fall. The newest month is partial until it ends (partial_month). An attack hit or carried one or more of these at once: it is counted under each, so these do not add up to the attacks.
- **Ask:** MCP `databank(category="health", indicator="who_ssa.attacks")` · REST `/v2/databank/health?indicator=who_ssa.attacks`
